HOW THIS WAS COLLECTED
----------------------
* NOTHING in the WorldBox install directory was modified. Read-only operations only.
* [System.Reflection.Assembly]::LoadFrom() FAILED on NeoModLoader.dll:
    "Could not load file or assembly 'file:///...NeoModLoader.dll' or one of its
     dependencies. Operation is not supported. (Exception from HRESULT: 0x80131515)"
  (the .NET "load from remote/untrusted location" restriction).
  Fallback used: Mono.Cecil metadata + IL disassembly, using
  NML\Assemblies\Mono.Cecil.dll (shipped by the loader itself).
* Assembly-CSharp.dll could not be loaded into PowerShell either (Newtonsoft.Json
  strong-name validation failed, 0x8013141A - the game ships a re-signed
  Newtonsoft.Json). Cecil was used for it as well.
* .NET metadata strings are UTF-16LE. ASCII extraction of Assembly-CSharp.dll
  yields 0 hits for "trait_name_" etc.; Unicode extraction is required.
* Upstream source downloaded read-only into:
    _tools\src        (master 36a891b5dcd067be43aa12574338d849c2947001)
    _tools\src-pinned (commit 02266fe8185d940c4065185de0a117c18975cf09)
    _tools\wiki       (git clone of https://github.com/WorldBoxOpenMods/ModLoader.wiki.git)

--- NeoModLoader.services.ModCompileLoadService::compileMod  (source collection + emit) ---
  (full dump in _tools\il_compile.txt, 1154 lines; key excerpts below)
  file filter  : name ends with ".cs" and does not start with "."
  dir  filter  : not starting with "." and not in Paths.IgnoreSearchDirectories
  search root  : the WHOLE mod folder (pModDecl.FolderPath), recursive
  parse        : CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest, preprocessorSymbols: <deps>)
  compilation  : CSharpCompilation::Create(UID, syntaxTrees, references, CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary))
  emit         : Paths.CompiledModsPath + UID + ".dll" / ".pdb"
  resources    : EmbededResources files -> manifest name "{ModName}.Resources.{relative path, separators -> .}"
  precompiled  : Directory::GetFiles(FolderPath, "*.dll") -> compilation skipped, ModType = COMPILED_NEOMOD
  load         : Assembly::Load(ReadAllBytes(dll), ReadAllBytes(pdb))
--- NeoModLoader.api.ModDeclare::.ctor(String pFilePath)  (mod.json parsing + UID rules) ---
  Newtonsoft.Json.JsonConvert::DeserializeObject<ModDeclare>(json)
  this.UID = deserialized.UID        (JSON key "GUID" via [JsonProperty("GUID")])
  if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(this.UID)) this.UID = this.Author + "." + this.Name;
  this.UID = ModDependencyUtils::ParseDepenNameToPreprocessSymbol(this.UID)
      -> upper-cases letters/digits, replaces every other char with _
  NOTE: TargetGameBuild is NOT copied in this constructor -> mod.json targetGameBuild
        is IGNORED for NML mods (only NCMSCompatibleLayer reads it).
  (full dump: _tools\il_moddeclare.txt)

--- NeoModLoader.utils.ModInfoUtils::recogMod  (how mod.json is located) ---
  folder -> mod.json in folder root; if absent, recursive search for mod.json in the folder
  -> new NeoModLoader.api.ModDeclare(modJsonPath)
  (full dump: _tools\il_recog.txt)
--- Newtonsoft.Json DefaultContractResolver.CreateProperty / ReflectionUtils.CanSetMemberValue ---
  Writable = CanSetMemberValue(member, hasMemberAttribute, canSetReadOnly)
  CanSetMemberValue(PropertyInfo, nonPublic, canSetReadOnly):
     if (!CanWrite) return false;
     if (nonPublic) return true;
     return GetSetMethod(nonPublic) != null;
  => a property with a PRIVATE setter is writable only when it carries a [JsonProperty]-style
     attribute (HasMemberAttribute == true). ModDeclare.UID has [JsonProperty("GUID")],
     which is exactly why the JSON key "GUID" reaches the private setter set_UID().

  ldarg.0        
  ldfld          System.String GodPower::name
  call           System.String StringExtension::Underscore(System.String)
  ret            
----- GodPower::getDescriptionID -----
  ldarg.0        
  call           System.String GodPower::getLocaleID()
  ldstr          "_description"
  call           System.String System.String::Concat(System.String,System.String)
  ret            
----- GodPower::getTranslatedName -----
  ldarg.0        
  call           System.String GodPower::getLocaleID()
  call           System.String StringExtension::Localize(System.String)
  ret

--- Mods\INVASION_SUMMONER\mod.json ---
{
  "name": "Invasion Summoner",
  "author": "you",
  "version": "0.46.15",
  "description": "v0.46.13: ZOOM RESYNC - the game draws units / buildings / trees only while QualityChanger.isLowRes() is false and that flag only follows the last setZoomOrthographic value; if it goes stale the map stays flat while zoomed in. The mod now re-syncs it with the real camera size every 0.2 s (Main.ZoomResync). ZOOM - the mod no longer touches the game's camera at all (zoom guard hooks and the camera write-back are off by default; Main.ZoomGuardEnabled). A new line under the mod's ms/frame label shows the camera size, fps and the game's own 'gameplay render' flag, and the log gets a [Render] line whenever that flag flips (to find out why the map goes flat). Side effect: the mouse wheel over the mod's panel also zooms the map. v0.46.12: ZOOM / INVISIBLE UNITS. The mod draws its map overlays with IMGUI, i.e. on top of the game's sprites. The dungeon circle fill now really fades out as you zoom in (it was a constant 20 % tint over the whole screen once the circle was bigger than the view) and the guild quarter markers are see-through. The game's own unit visibility (ZoneCamera / updateVisibility) is not touched by the mod. Test: the 'Circles: ON/OFF' button next to the panel switches all of this off. v0.46.11: THREAD SAFETY (verified in the game's BatchActors code): Actor.updateStats runs in the game's parallel job phase on worker threads, so the mod's stat hook now only touches thread-safe data - the reflection member cache it uses is locked (an unlocked Dictionary written by two worker threads can hang the game). The attack checks (findEnemyTarget / checkCurrentEnemyTarget) run in the main-thread phase and were already safe. v0.46.10: BIG-WORLD / LONG-RUN AUDIT. The Guilds and Ranking tabs drew and re-sorted EVERY guild on every GUI event (guild count is unlimited): they now list the 40 / 100 strongest guilds from a list sorted once a second, and the live level refresh only re-reads the guilds that are drawn. A guild's armory keeps at most 25 spare weapons (it grew with every cleared dungeon and the equip scan is nested). Dead members are also dropped from the training table, and the per-guild upkeep no longer allocates a list per guild per pass. The game's attack-check / level-lock / stat hooks (the game can call them from worker threads) now swallow any lookup that races the mod's own edits instead of letting an exception escape into the game. v0.46.9: BIG POPULATIONS (1000+ units). The windows no longer walk every hunter on every GUI event: the Strongest hunters list is built once a second, an opened guild lists its strongest 40 members (sorted list cached for 1 s), guild tabs re-read levels once a second (was 5x), the Market rich-list every 3 s. A [Load] line is written to the mod log every 2 minutes (units, hunters, guilds, dungeons, monsters, fps, load scale, lag guard) so the behaviour at a given population can be checked. v0.46.8: LOWEST UNIT CHECKING. Every recurring per-unit scan was cut to a small slice at a long gap, and exits at once when there is nothing to check: membership purge 1500 -> 60 units/s (and only while a dungeon monster rules a city / kingdom), auto-favorite 250 -> 20/s, backup shield 400 -> 50/s, level lock 300 -> 40/s, cleanup 300 -> 50/s (snapshot every 20 s), monster calming 270 -> 50/s, rampage 36 -> 10 monsters/s with 8 look-ups each (was 24), hunter enlisting up to 500 -> 80 units/s, talent recruiting 200 -> 40/s, guild-candidate scan 3000 per 2 s -> 800 per 8 s, member level read-back every 10-16 s (was 4-7), circle leash 400 -> 160 monsters/s (list rebuilt every 3 s), market sweep every 3 s (3 ms, 1500 units max; was every 1 s, 6 ms, 6000). All of it still multiplies with the load scale in big worlds. v0.46.7: LONG-RUN / BIG-WORLD OPTIMISATION (ideas taken from the PopOpt crowd optimiser, own code): a LOAD SCALE (1x for small worlds up to 4x with 10,000+ units, doubled while the lag guard is active) spaces out the mod's recurring unit scans (level lock, cleanup, calm / rampage, hunter enlisting, talent recruiting, level read-back, guild tasks; the dungeon and market tasks keep their pace); the unit list is refilled into two reused lists instead of a new large list every second (no more large-object-heap garbage); the talent cache is capped (it kept every unit ever scanned, dead ones too). v0.46.6: the dungeon circle is like a kingdom: filled with its colour (20 %) and a clear border, and it is clipped to the map area so it is never drawn over the game's bottom bar / tabs (hidden as before while a game window is open). LAG GUARD (below 11 fps for 1.5 s the mod spaces out its stages, runs the guild tick twice a second and hides the map markers until the game recovers), the zoom guard only hooks the wheel / input-level zoom methods (never the ones the game calls itself) and no longer writes the camera size back, map tags are built twice a second instead of on every GUI event. FIX the dungeon circle hid buildings and units when zoomed in - it was one flat tint drawn over the whole map; now it is a thin ring (constant 3 px) plus a very light fill (max 12 %) that fades out as you zoom in, and the Death Watch log line also records the camera zoom. Kings and city leaders can no longer enlist as hunters, be recruited as talents, or be picked as battle casualties (they used to leave their kingdom and die in dungeons); clearing a monster's target now calls the game's own clearAttackTarget (the game keeps a separate has_attack_target flag). v0.46.5: the Guilds tab shows the number of active registered hunters (guild members + solo hunters + mercenaries). v0.46.4: YEARLY HUNTER ENLISTMENT - any unit aged 18 or more (not only those who just turned 18) may try to register as a hunter once per in-game year; each try succeeds with EnlistChance (50%, Guilds.cs) and a failed try waits until the next year; A-SSS talents still go straight to a guild. v0.46.3: DEATH WATCH (DeathWatch.cs) - logs a sudden drop of living units with its likely causes (wars, broken dungeons, frame time, speed) and shows the last one on the Invade tab; diagnostic only. v0.46.2: FREEZE GUARD (PerfGuard.cs) - every per-frame stage of the mod (level lock, cleanup, calm/shield, rampage, notices, spawning, walls, button queue) is timed; a stage slower than 35 ms is logged as [Perf] and spaced out (0.25 s doubling to 8 s), back to normal after 60 s without a slow run. v0.46.1: PREVENT WAR now uses the game's real methods (verified in Assembly-CSharp.dll): DiplomacyHelpers.isWarNeeded / getWarTarget, DiplomacyManager.startWar / startTotalWar, WarManager.newWar are blocked and WarManager.stopAllWars ends running wars; toggle in the Invade tab (on by default). v0.45.33: allocation is now staged per guild (nearest / rank-qualified first): 1) its own average combat power must be enough, 2) otherwise it hires mercenaries, 3) otherwise it forms an alliance with other guilds' squads, 4) otherwise the dungeon passes to the next guild. v0.45.32: dungeon allocation - a dungeon is offered to the guilds one by one (guilds whose rank reaches the dungeon rank first, the nearest first, then by rank); the first guild that can clear it with its own squads (hiring mercenaries if short) takes it, otherwise it passes to the next guild (flag AllocateByRankAndDistance). v0.45.31: expeditions are topped up to the dungeon's attacker limit (extra idle squads of the same guilds first, then free solo hunters / mercenaries, no fodder under half the party average), and when several squads are strong enough the one of the highest-rank guild goes, so higher-rank guilds conquer lower-tier dungeons (flags FillToCap / HigherGuildsFirst in Guilds.cs). v0.45.30: GUI - explanatory notes removed from the Hunters, Market, News and Broken tabs, windows made narrower, and a Solo Leveling style system look (dark navy panels, cyan frames, [ SYSTEM ] titles). v0.45.29: the SSS market trait crystal can also give Qi Condensation (realm_01_qi). v0.45.28: the '?' trait (the_mysterious_trait) removed from the SSS reward / trait crystal pool and banned from rewards. v0.45.27: Madness Intent (intent_09_madness) is back in the D reward pool; only the plain madness trait stays banned from dungeon rewards and trait crystals. v0.45.26: (the madness trait is never a dungeon reward or a trait crystal) v0.45.25: last stand = the strongest FREE squads / solo hunters (ranked by power) march until the attacker limit; if none is free the strongest free unit goes in alone. v0.45.24: a dungeon breaks when its years run out even while hunters are attacking it; a failed attempt no longer makes the dungeon rest (the next challenger goes in at once); the last stand no longer waits for every guild to be idle, so every dungeon gets an attacker. v0.45.23: war freeze - the attack-check patch rejects units that have nothing to do with a dungeon after two lookups (no MethodBase per call, separate entry for isEnemyTargetOf), the membership patch only hooks the known join / king / leader setters (the name-guessing net hit methods that run constantly in wars), and the monster-place rebuild runs every 4 s. v0.45.22: spirit roots and dao foundations removed from the reward / trait crystal pools. v0.45.21: Xianni traits added to the reward / trait crystal pools of every grade (divine arts, immortal arts, intents, spirit roots and dao foundations; no cultivation realms, no ancient / beast stages, no path or element traits, no lifespan-cost or revive traits). v0.45.20: more reward traits / trait crystals for every grade (E 11, D 11, C 8, B 8, A 9, S 7, SS 5, SSS 3) from Avb's Ultra Trait Pack, Darkie Custom Traits and the base game; no summon / clone / convert traits. v0.45.19: squad rank fixed - a squad is rated by its plain average CP against a fixed CP per rank (E 250, D 520, C 4,100, B 8,600, A 50,000, S 100,000, SS 400,000, SSS 2,100,000; table SquadCpByRank in Guilds.cs); the same bar decides which squad is sent to a dungeon, mercenary hiring, and a guild's team rank. v0.45.18: a unit solos a dungeon once its own CP reaches a fixed CP per rank (E 400, D 850, C 6,500, B 14,000, A 80,000, S 160,000, SS 650,000, SSS 3,400,000; table SoloCpByRank in Guilds.cs); used for actual entry, the Hunters tab, auto-favorite and guild rank. v0.45.17: guild rank, 'can solo up to' and squad rank now use a FIXED dungeon power table measured in game (E 90-250, D 260-520, C 600-4,100, B 1,800-8,600, A 15,000-50,000, S 40,000-100,000, SS 110,000-400,000, SSS 860,000-2,100,100; reference = geometric mean of each range). v0.45.16: war performance - the attack-check patch no longer allocates per call and exits at once when no dungeon unit exists; the membership patch exits at once when no place is ruled by a dungeon monster. v0.45.15: FIX mass death - cities and kingdoms that merely lie under a dungeon circle are no longer stripped of their king, leader and citizens (only places ruled by a dungeon monster are protected). v0.45.14: guild training is x10 (was x8) the natural experience of the member's own talent rank. v0.45.13: the Level Scroll +1 was removed from the Market. v0.45.12: monsters of an intact dungeon only fight the hunters sent against it (villagers inside the circle are no longer attacked), with a backup sweep that does not depend on the attack-check patches. v0.45.11: performance - reflection lookups (kill, calm, goTo, traits) are cached instead of enumerating every game method per call, and the mod holds back its background work while the game runs under ~8 fps. v0.45.10: every squad shows the dungeon rank it can handle; solo hunters need 1.35x the old bar before entering a dungeon (and the Hunters tab uses the same bar, measured from their real current power). v0.45.9: a guild can send several of its own squads together to a dungeon (Squad 1 + Squad 2) when no single squad is strong enough, before squads of different guilds team up. v0.45.8: materials sell to the Market for 80% of their value (was 10%). v0.45.7: anti-freeze pass - unit alive checks are remembered for ~0.12 s (they were repeated tens of thousands of times a second), guild maintenance is time-sliced (4 ms per call), guild recruiting shares one candidate scan and is time-boxed, hunter enlisting builds its guild list once per pass and stops after 8 ms, solo hunters settling look at 12 random guilds instead of measuring every guild. v0.45.6: materials can be sold to the Market in any amount (1, 10, all): as many units as Player Earnings can pay for. v0.45.5: guilds, solo hunters and mercenaries automatically sell their materials to the Market (the player) whenever Player Earnings can pay for the whole stack; the coins are taken from Player Earnings. v0.45.4: guild treasuries are no longer topped up to 100 coins; a guild only shops in the Market while at least 100 coins remain after the purchase (kept for hiring mercenaries). v0.45.3: cleanup - removed unused code, frame-time clamp so a freeze cannot fast-forward fights and kill units, lighter GUI. v0.45.2: Market fix - the performance breaker can no longer switch the Market off for the session (it recovers on its own after 30 s, plus a restore button in the Market tab), and the Market tab no longer re-reads every unit 5x a second. v0.45.1: PERMANENT GUILD SQUADS. Every guild is divided into attack squads of up to 8 units (strongest units = Squad 1, then Squad 2, ...; sizes are balanced; idle squads are re-sorted about once a year, squads that are away are never touched). Hierarchy: Guild Master > Vice > Squad Leaders > Deputy Squad Leaders > Soldiers (leader = strongest of the squad, deputy = second; a fallen leader is replaced by the next strongest). A dungeon is now attacked by SQUADS, not by whole guilds: a guild with several idle squads attacks several dungeons at once (one squad per dungeon when it is strong enough, otherwise several squads / squads of several guilds team up, with mercenaries as a temporary mercenary squad). The strongest squad leader of an expedition is its Expedition Commander (name tag CMD); name tags show |SL# |DSL# |SQ#. Followers march behind their leader and casualties fall soldiers-first, commander-last. The guild window lists the squads and where they are; the unit panel shows the unit's squad role; guild status shows how many squads are out. Squads of a guild that is out on one dungeon no longer block its other squads, and a guild recruits while squads are away. v0.45.0 (squads were temporary then): mercenary fee now 20 (weakest) to 500 (strongest). Dungeon attacker limit now 8 (E) to 45 (SSS): 8, 13, 19, 24, 29, 34, 40, 45. ATTACK SQUADS: an expedition is organised into squads of up to 8 (each guild fields its own, mercenaries form mercenary squads) with a hierarchy: Expedition Commander > Squad Leaders > Deputy Squad Leaders > Soldiers (by combat power). Followers march behind their leader, casualties fall in reverse rank order (soldiers first, commander last), a fallen leader is replaced by the next strongest, roles show in the name tag ([..|CMD], |SL2, |DSL2, |SQ2), the unit panel and the dungeon window. v0.44.9: every guild treasury keeps 100 coins for hiring mercenaries (guild shopping never spends below 100, and a guild under 100 is topped back up to 100). The mercenary commission now depends on the mercenary's combat power (10 coins at E-boss level up to 400 at SSS-boss level, interpolated); a guild that cannot pay a mercenary skips him and tries a cheaper one; the fee is shown on the mercenary's unit panel. v0.44.8: no buying restrictions. Units and guilds can buy any tier up to SSS (the coins are the only limit); level scrolls are useful up to Lv 999; guild treasuries no longer keep a mercenary reserve, no longer skip while on a dungeon, and no longer favour their own rank. A guild shops only for members who really need something, strongest combat power first down to the weakest: pass 1 = better weapon / armor / accessory / trait crystal, pass 2 (only when nobody needs gear or a trait the treasury can pay for) = level scrolls. v0.44.7: units buy constantly. Every unit (guild members, solo hunters, mercenaries) is swept in order once a second (time-boxed to 6 ms) instead of a few random units every 3 s; a unit shops as soon as it can pay the cheapest item (no 20-coin minimum, no 20% reserve), also while its guild is on a dungeon; it may buy any tier it can pay for (not only guild rank + 1); level scrolls keep being useful until a rank above what the unit can solo; a failed game item pauses gear buying for ~3 s instead of ~15 s; guilds shop for members every 3-8 s and try 10 members. v0.44.6: 'can solo up to N-rank' is now accurate. It uses the exact bar the game applies to a lone hunter (boss CP x margin / whole-dungeon CP x readiness), taken from a real dungeon of that rank on the map when there is one, else from the measured average, else an estimate marked ~ (interpolated between measured ranks with a learned growth exponent). A rank is only claimed if every easier rank is also soloable (the old check could claim S while skipping D), a fresh dungeon counts its boss (+1 monster), and the hunters tab shows the CP needed for the current and the next rank. v0.44.5: Market. Guild treasury purchases for members now appear in Market > Transactions (buyer = the guild, 'for <member>', remaining = treasury); the history keeps 10 entries. The Market tab shows why units are or are not buying (hunters checked / bought / skipped: guild on a dungeon, native Coins unreadable, under 20 coins, nothing useful, failed, last failure; same for guild treasuries; warns if the performance breaker slowed or switched the Market off) and the same line is written to the mod log every minute. The guild shopping pass is time-boxed to 4 ms so it cannot trip that breaker. v0.44.3: dungeon base coins are now E 150, D 300, C 600, B 1200, A 2400, S 4800, SS 9600, SSS 19200 (150 for the lowest, doubling every rank), times the 0.75-1.5 difficulty multiplier. v0.44.2: dungeon coin rewards follow the dungeon's difficulty (rank base x 0.75-1.5 from its real combat power). The guild takes 25% into its treasury, the other 75% is split between the hunters who fought by contribution (combat power); lone hunters / mercenaries without a guild keep everything. The guild treasury now pays for mercenaries (commission) and, automatically, for weapons / armor / accessories / trait crystals for its members (it keeps a reserve for 2 mercenary hires first). v0.43.16: Market prices and stock. Weapons / armor / accessories cost 20 (E) up to 450 (SSS) (20, 35, 60, 100, 160, 250, 350, 450); trait crystals 30 (E) up to 600 (SSS) (30, 55, 95, 150, 240, 360, 480, 600); level scrolls +1 = 10 coins, +5 = 45, +10 = 85, +25 = 200, +50 = 385, +100 = 750; every item has unlimited stock. v0.43.15: dungeons now break on time. Cause: DungeonTick handled the dungeons hardest-first in one loop, and an error in any dungeon stopped the loop, so the dungeons at the end of the list (the E rank ones) never reached their 'break after N years', assignment or last-stand code (age 55y / 20y but still 'Waiting for a strong enough guild'). Every dungeon is now processed on its own inside a try/catch (errors are logged once as '[Dungeon] error while processing ...' and the break check still runs), and Break() announces and marks the break even if spawning the overflow monsters fails. Break times by rank are unchanged: E 20y, D 25y, C 30y, B 40y, A 50y, S 65y, SS 80y, SSS 100y. v0.43.14: hardening. (1) Circuit breaker: any mod task that takes over 250 ms in a frame is slowed down and, if it keeps happening (3 times, or once over 2 s), switched OFF for the session with a [Perf] line in the log, so one runaway task can no longer freeze the game. (2) Dungeon fights only kill units the mod itself made hostile / tracks, never stray world units. (3) The automatic features are ON again (guilds, dungeons, Market shopping, auto-favorite). v0.43.12: diagnostics only (no gameplay change): the mod log now records every dungeon fight ([Fight] rank, name, solo/party, win chance, WIN/LOSS) and any mod task that takes over 80 ms in a frame ([Perf] task N took X ms), to find what freezes the game / kills the hunters. v0.43.11: unit panel Select / Support buttons rebuilt: a small clean row BELOW the unit window (Select/Unselect + Support 100) with a status line under it. The Select label switches to Unselect, buttons grey out for units that are not guild members / hunters / mercenaries (before, clicking on them silently did nothing), and every click shows feedback (selected, supported, not enough Player Earnings, ...). Support now reports real success/failure instead of ignoring it. v0.43.10: item descriptions now show exactly the origin text: dungeon loot reads 'Pit of Bones (E)' and Market purchases read 'The Market (SSS-Grade Weapon)' (Armor / Accessory for those kinds). The game's description (Item.getItemDescription, checked in Assembly-CSharp.dll) is built from ItemData.by / ItemData.from inside a 'Created by ... of ...' template, so v0.43.9 produced 'Created by Dungeon loot of Pit of Bones (E)'; it is now patched to return only the origin text for items made by this mod. v0.43.9: item origin text format: dungeon loot reads 'Pit of Bones (E)' (dungeon name + difficulty) and Market purchases read 'The Market (SSS-Grade Weapon)' (Armor / Accessory for those kinds). v0.43.8: item descriptions now include the dungeon difficulty for dungeon loot (e.g. 'B-rank <dungeon>') and the grade for Market purchases (e.g. 'the Market (S-grade weapon)'). v0.43.7: units can keep buying in the Market when they already have gear: a stronger weapon replaces the old one (the old weapon goes back to the armory), and a stronger armor / accessory replaces their weakest worn piece when every slot is taken. v0.43.6: the description of every dungeon-loot weapon / armor / accessory says 'Dungeon loot' and names the dungeon it came from; Market-bought items say 'Market purchase' (the game item description reads ItemData.by / ItemData.from). v0.43.5: native items are now connected to this game build's real item system (read from Assembly-CSharp.dll): items are EquipmentAsset ids like sword_iron / helmet_copper / ring_bone, made with MapBox.items.generateItem exactly like the game's own crafting, and worn through Actor.equipment slots (verified with isEmpty()). v0.43.4: the Market tab now shows whether native item creation/equipping is connected (like native Coins); item creation falls back to hand-building the ItemData, equipping tries every equip/give method of the unit, and the real item API of the game build is written to the log ([ItemAPI] lines) on the first failure. v0.43.3: removed dagger / mace / staff (not native WorldBox weapons). Market weapons, armor and accessories are now only sold when the real game item is created AND really equipped on the unit (otherwise the purchase is cancelled, nothing is charged, and the reason is written to the mod log); item creation now passes the world to ItemGenerator and falls back to the base material; a unit whose bought item disappears can buy again. v0.43.2: News now also lists guilds / alliances / solo hunters assembling for a dungeon (incl. last stands) and their defeats, each lasting 1 year. v0.43.1: removed the recruiting and Market-purchase lines from the on-screen banners and the mod game log (Market purchases remain in the Market Transactions tab). v0.43.0: the Log tab is replaced by a News tab (dungeon conquests by guilds / solo hunters, guild promotions, dungeon breaks; E-A dungeon news and promotions fade after 1 year, S-SSS dungeon news stays forever). v0.42.1: smoothness pass - the 0.5 s guild/dungeon tick is now time-sliced (one small task per frame), attack checks use cached positions, leash/purge/market/favorite work is budgeted. v0.42: Market has separate Stock, Transactions, and Support a Unit tabs. Unit panel has Select/Unselect and Support buttons; selected unit carries into Market direct gear/trait purchases. Market Transactions keeps only the 10 latest activities. Records NPC/autonomous purchases, player Coin support transfers to units, and player-funded gear/trait purchases for a selected unit. Player-funded gear/traits are actually equipped/granted to that unit. Market uses native WorldBox Coins, autonomous buying, immediate stock, 10-year restock, contribution rewards, and A-SSS Qi Condensation-only dungeon cultivation rewards.",
  "targetGameBuild": 115,
  "GUID": "INVASION_SUMMONER"
}

--- Mods\SCPBOX_MOD\Code\ScpLocale.cs (first 55 lines) : the LM localization pattern ---
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using HarmonyLib;
using NCMS.Utils;
using NeoModLoader.General;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace ScpBox
{
    // Localization management and dictionary synchronization
    public static class ScpLocale
    {
        private static readonly Dictionary<string, string> _translations =
            new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        public static void Initialize()
        {
            Text("ScpBox", "Foundation");
            Text("scp_tab_desc", "Anomalies, containment Sites and the groups that fight over them.");

            Units();
            Buildings();
            Traits();
            Statuses();
            Powers();
            Site();

            ApplyAll();
        }

        public static void ApplyAll()
        {
            if (LocalizedTextManager.instance != null && LocalizedTextManager.instance._localized_text != null)
            {
                foreach (var kvp in _translations)
                {
                    LocalizedTextManager.instance._localized_text[kvp.Key] = kvp.Value;
                }
            }

            foreach (var kvp in _translations)
            {
                try { LM.AddToCurrentLocale(kvp.Key, kvp.Value); } catch { }
                try { LM.Add("en", kvp.Key, kvp.Value); } catch { }
            }

            try { LM.ApplyLocale(false); } catch { }
        }

        public static bool TryGetTranslation(string pKey, out string pValue)
        {
            pValue = null;
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(pKey)) return false;

  ...
        private static ActorTrait Make(string pId, string pIcon)
        {
            ActorTrait existing = AssetManager.traits.get(pId);
            if (existing != null) return existing;

            ActorTrait trait = new ActorTrait
            {
                id = pId,
                path_icon = "ui/Icons/" + pIcon,
                group_id = "special",
                rate_birth = 0,
                rate_inherit = 0,
                rate_acquire_grow_up = 0,
                spawn_random_trait_allowed = false,
                can_be_cured = false,
                can_be_removed_by_divine_light = false,
                is_mutation_box_allowed = false
            };
            AssetManager.traits.add(trait);
            return trait;
        }

        private static void Tick(ActorTrait pTrait, WorldAction pAction, float pInterval)
        {
            pTrait.action_special_effect = (WorldAction)Delegate.Combine(pTrait.action_special_effect, pAction);

--- Mods\SCPBOX_MOD\Code\Main.cs (first 55 lines) : BasicMod entry point ---
using System;
using System.Reflection;
using HarmonyLib;
using NCMS;
using UnityEngine;
using NeoModLoader.api;

namespace ScpBox
{
    [ModEntry]
    class Main : BasicMod<Main>
    {
        public static Main instance;
        public static string ModPath;

        // Path to mod directory
        public static string ModFolder
        {
            get
            {
                string baseDir = ModPath ?? AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory;
                string modsDir = System.IO.Path.Combine(baseDir, "Mods");
                if (System.IO.Directory.Exists(modsDir))
                {
                    foreach (string dir in System.IO.Directory.GetDirectories(modsDir))
                    {
                        if (System.IO.Path.GetFileName(dir).ToLower().Contains("scpbox")) return dir;
                    }
                }
                return baseDir;
            }
        }

        private const float SLOW_TICK = 1f;
        private const float REBUILD_TICK = 30f;

        private float _slowTimer;
        private float _rebuildTimer;
        private double _lastWorldTime = -1.0;

        protected override void OnModLoad()
        {
            instance = this;
            ModPath = AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory;

            Config.show_console_on_error = false;
            ScpUtils.Log("loading...");

            Stage("settings", ModSettings.Initialize);
            Stage("registry", AnomalyRegistry.Initialize);
            Stage("factions", ScpFactions.Initialize);
            Stage("statuses", ScpStatuses.Initialize);
            Stage("traits", ScpTraits.Initialize);
            Stage("anomalies", Anomalies.Initialize);
            Stage("faction units", FoundationUnits.Initialize);

--- api/ModDeclare.cs  (the authoritative mod.json schema) ---
using System.Reflection;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using NeoModLoader.General;
using NeoModLoader.services;
using NeoModLoader.utils;
using Newtonsoft.Json;
using UnityEngine;

namespace NeoModLoader.api;

/// <summary>
/// Mod type, determine which mod loader to load it.
/// </summary>
public enum ModTypeEnum
{
    /// <summary>
    /// NeoMod
    /// </summary>
    NEOMOD,

    /// <summary>
    ///     Compiled NeoMod
    /// </summary>
    COMPILED_NEOMOD,

    /// <summary>
    /// BepInEx
    /// </summary>
    BEPINEX,

    /// <summary>
    ///     Resource Pack
    /// </summary>
    RESOURCE_PACK
}

internal enum ModState
{
    DISABLED,
    LOADED,
    FAILED
}

/// <summary>
/// Declaration of a mod.
/// </summary>
[Serializable]
public class ModDeclare
{
#pragma warning disable CS8618
    ModDeclare()
#pragma warning restore CS8618
    {
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Create a ModDeclare object with given parameters
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="pName"></param>
    /// <param name="pAuthor"></param>
    /// <param name="pIconPath"></param>
    /// <param name="pVersion"></param>
    /// <param name="pDescription"></param>
    /// <param name="pFolderPath"></param>
    /// <param name="pDependencies"></param>
    /// <param name="pOptionalDependencies"></param>
    /// <param name="pIncompatibleWith"></param>
    /// <param name="pIsWorkshopLoaded"></param>
    public ModDeclare(string pName, string pAuthor, string pIconPath, string pVersion, string pDescription,
        string pFolderPath, string[] pDependencies, string[] pOptionalDependencies, string[] pIncompatibleWith,
        bool pIsWorkshopLoaded = false)
    {
        Name = pName;
        Author = pAuthor;
        IconPath = pIconPath;
        Version = pVersion;
        Description = pDescription;
        Dependencies = pDependencies ?? Array.Empty<string>();
        OptionalDependencies = pOptionalDependencies ?? Array.Empty<string>();
        IncompatibleWith = pIncompatibleWith ?? Array.Empty<string>();
        IsWorkshopLoaded = pIsWorkshopLoaded;

        UID = ModDependencyUtils.ParseDepenNameToPreprocessSymbol($"{Author}.{Name}");

        for (int i = 0; i < Dependencies.Length; i++)
            Dependencies[i] = ModDependencyUtils.ParseDepenNameToPreprocessSymbol(Dependencies[i]);
        for (int i = 0; i < OptionalDependencies.Length; i++)
            OptionalDependencies[i] = ModDependencyUtils.ParseDepenNameToPreprocessSymbol(OptionalDependencies[i]);
        for (int i = 0; i < IncompatibleWith.Length; i++)
            IncompatibleWith[i] = ModDependencyUtils.ParseDepenNameToPreprocessSymbol(IncompatibleWith[i]);

        FolderPath = pFolderPath;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Read a mod config file and parse it into a ModDeclare object
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="pFilePath"></param>
    /// <exception cref="InvalidOperationException"></exception>
    /// <exception cref="Exception"></exception>
    public ModDeclare(string pFilePath)
    {
        ModDeclare modDeclare = JsonConvert.DeserializeObject<ModDeclare>(File.ReadAllText(pFilePath)) ??
                                throw new InvalidOperationException("Input Mod Config file path cannot be null");
        if (modDeclare == null)
        {
            throw new Exception($"Mod Config file at \"{pFilePath}\" is invalid");
        }

        UpdateFrom(modDeclare);

        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(UID)) UID = $"{Author}.{Name}";
        UID = ModDependencyUtils.ParseDepenNameToPreprocessSymbol(UID);

        for (int i = 0; i < Dependencies.Length; i++)
            Dependencies[i] = ModDependencyUtils.ParseDepenNameToPreprocessSymbol(Dependencies[i]);
        for (int i = 0; i < OptionalDependencies.Length; i++)
            OptionalDependencies[i] = ModDependencyUtils.ParseDepenNameToPreprocessSymbol(OptionalDependencies[i]);
        for (int i = 0; i < IncompatibleWith.Length; i++)
            IncompatibleWith[i] = ModDependencyUtils.ParseDepenNameToPreprocessSymbol(IncompatibleWith[i]);

        FolderPath = Path.GetDirectoryName(pFilePath) ??
                     throw new Exception("Cannot get folder path from input file path");
        var pathSegments = FolderPath.Split(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar);
        int currentSearchIndex = pathSegments.IndexOf("workshop");
        if (currentSearchIndex == -1) return;
        if (currentSearchIndex + 3 >= pathSegments.Length) return;
        if (pathSegments[++currentSearchIndex] != "content") return;
        if (pathSegments[++currentSearchIndex] != "1206560" /* workshop ID of WorldBox */) return;
        Regex regex = new(@"^\d+$");
        if (!regex.IsMatch(pathSegments[++currentSearchIndex])) return;
        IsWorkshopLoaded = true;
        if (SteamId == 0 && ulong.TryParse(pathSegments[currentSearchIndex], out ulong parsedId))
        {
            SteamId = parsedId;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Refresh metadata while retaining references held by rows and dependency nodes.</summary>
    internal void UpdateFrom(ModDeclare pModDeclare)
    {
        Name = pModDeclare.Name;
        Author = pModDeclare.Author;
        Version = pModDeclare.Version;
        IconPath = pModDeclare.IconPath;
        Description = pModDeclare.Description;
        Dependencies = pModDeclare.Dependencies;
        OptionalDependencies = pModDeclare.OptionalDependencies;
        IncompatibleWith = pModDeclare.IncompatibleWith;
        ModType = pModDeclare.ModType;
        UsePublicizedAssembly = pModDeclare.UsePublicizedAssembly;
        RepoUrl = pModDeclare.RepoUrl;
        AIAttribution = pModDeclare.AIAttribution ?? "not_ai";
        AIBadgeCorner = pModDeclare.AIBadgeCorner ?? "bottom_right";
        AIBadgeStyle = pModDeclare.AIBadgeStyle ?? "standard";
        AICheckCode = pModDeclare.AICheckCode;
        AICheckArt = pModDeclare.AICheckArt;
        AICheckTranslation = pModDeclare.AICheckTranslation;
        AICheckAudio = pModDeclare.AICheckAudio;
        Tags = pModDeclare.Tags ?? new List<string>();
        SteamId = pModDeclare.SteamId;

        Dependencies ??= Array.Empty<string>();
        OptionalDependencies ??= Array.Empty<string>();
        IncompatibleWith ??= Array.Empty<string>();

        UID = pModDeclare.UID;
        FolderPath = pModDeclare.FolderPath;
        IsWorkshopLoaded = pModDeclare.IsWorkshopLoaded;
        IsNCMSMod = pModDeclare.IsNCMSMod;
        TargetGameBuild = pModDeclare.TargetGameBuild;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Mod Name. Add locale of $"{Name}_{language}" can make it display different name in different language
    /// </summary>
    [JsonProperty("name")]
    public string Name { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>
    /// Unique ID. GUID.ToValid() or $"{Author}_{Name}".ToUpper().ToValid(). ToValid: Replace all characters belong to ASCII but are not letters or numbers with '_'
    /// </summary>
    [JsonProperty("GUID")]
    public string UID { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>
    /// Mod Author. Add locale of $"{Author}_{language}" can make it display different name in different language
    /// </summary>

    [JsonProperty("author")]
    public string Author { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>
    /// Mod Version
    /// </summary>
    [JsonProperty("version")]
    public string Version { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>
    /// Mod Description. Add locale of $"{Description}_{language}" can make it display different name in different language
    /// </summary>
    [JsonProperty("description")]
    public string Description { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>
    /// Url to repo or website of this mod.
    /// </summary>
    [JsonProperty("RepoUrl")]
    public string RepoUrl { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>
    /// List of hard dependencies' UID
    /// </summary>
    [JsonProperty("Dependencies")]
    public string[] Dependencies { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>
    /// List of soft dependencies' UID
    /// </summary>
    [JsonProperty("OptionalDependencies")]
    public string[] OptionalDependencies { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>
    /// List of incompatible mods' UID. Not implemented yet.
    /// </summary>
    [JsonProperty("IncompatibleWith")]
    public string[] IncompatibleWith { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>
    /// The mod's folder path.
    /// </summary>
    public string FolderPath { get; private set; } = null!;

    /// <summary>
    /// Target Game Build. Not implemented yet.
    /// </summary>
    [JsonProperty("targetGameBuild")]
    public int TargetGameBuild { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>
    /// Path to icon file. Relative to mod folder.
    /// </summary>
    [JsonProperty("iconPath")]
    public string IconPath { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>
    /// Mod type.
    /// </summary>
    [JsonProperty("ModType")]
    public ModTypeEnum ModType { get; private set; } = ModTypeEnum.NEOMOD;

    /// <summary>
    /// Whether to use publicized assembly.
    /// </summary>
    [JsonProperty("UsePublicizedAssembly")]
    public bool UsePublicizedAssembly { get; private set; } = true;

    /// <summary>
    /// Whether this mod has been identified as an NCMS mod.
    /// </summary>
    public bool IsNCMSMod { get; internal set; } = false;

    /// <summary>
    /// Reason of failing to compile or load.
    /// </summary>
    public StringBuilder FailReason { get; } = new();

    /// <summary>
    /// Whether the files of this mod were downloaded using the Steam workshop.
    /// </summary>
    public bool IsWorkshopLoaded { get; internal set; } = false;

    internal void SetRepoUrlToWorkshopPage(string id)
    {
        RepoUrl = $"https://steamcommunity.com/sharedfiles/filedetails/?id={id}";
    }

    internal void SetModType(ModTypeEnum modType)
    {
        if (modType < ModTypeEnum.NEOMOD || modType > ModTypeEnum.RESOURCE_PACK)
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(modType), modType, null);
        ModType = modType;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// This only called for BepInEx mods
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="iconPath"></param>
    internal void SetIconPath(string iconPath)
    {
        IconPath = iconPath;
    }
    public Sprite GetIcon()
    {
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(IconPath))
            return SpriteLoadUtils.LoadSingleSprite(Path.Combine(FolderPath, IconPath));
        return InternalResourcesGetter.GetIcon();
    }
    public string GetDisplayName()
    {
        var display_name = Name;
        var multilang_mod_name_key = $"{Name}_{LocalizedTextManager.instance.language}";
        if (LocalizedTextManager.stringExists(multilang_mod_name_key)) display_name = LM.Get(multilang_mod_name_key);
        return display_name;
    }
    public string GetDisplayAuthor()
    {
        var display_author = Author;
        var multilang_mod_author_key = $"{Author}_{LocalizedTextManager.instance.language}";
        if (LocalizedTextManager.stringExists(multilang_mod_author_key)) display_author = LM.Get(multilang_mod_author_key);
        return display_author;
    }
    /// <summary>Version for display, with one leading "v" and no surrounding whitespace.</summary>
    public string GetDisplayVersion()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Version)) return "";
        string version = Version.Trim();
        return version.StartsWith("v", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? version : "v" + version;
    }

    public string GetDisplayDesc()
    {
        var display_desc = Description;
        var multilang_mod_desc_key = $"{Description}_{LocalizedTextManager.instance.language}";
        if (LocalizedTextManager.stringExists(multilang_mod_desc_key)) display_desc = LM.Get(multilang_mod_desc_key);
        return display_desc;
    }

    [JsonProperty("ai_attribution")]
    public string AIAttribution { get; internal set; } = "not_ai";

    [JsonProperty("ai_badge_corner")]
    public string AIBadgeCorner { get; internal set; } = "bottom_right";

    [JsonProperty("ai_badge_style")]
    public string AIBadgeStyle { get; internal set; } = "standard";

    [JsonProperty("ai_check_code")]
    public bool AICheckCode { get; internal set; }

    [JsonProperty("ai_check_art")]
    public bool AICheckArt { get; internal set; }

    [JsonProperty("ai_check_translation")]
    public bool AICheckTranslation { get; internal set; }

    [JsonProperty("ai_check_audio")]
    public bool AICheckAudio { get; internal set; }

    public List<string> GetAIChecklistItems()
    {
        List<string> items = new List<string>();
        if (AICheckCode) items.Add("Code / Logic");
        if (AICheckArt) items.Add("Textures / Sprites");
        if (AICheckTranslation) items.Add("Translations / Text");
        if (AICheckAudio) items.Add("Audio / Music");
        return items;
    }

    public string GetAIAttributionDisplay()
    {
        return AIAttribution?.ToLower() switch
        {
            "ai_made" => "AI-Made",
            "ai_assisted" => "AI-Assisted",
            "not_ai" => "Human Authored",
            _ => "Unspecified"
        };
    }

    [JsonProperty("steam_id")]
    public ulong SteamId { get; internal set; }

    private List<string> _tags = new();

    [JsonProperty("tags")]
    public List<string> Tags
    {
        get => _tags ??= new List<string>();
        internal set => _tags = value ?? new List<string>();
    }

    public bool HasTag(string tag)
    {
        return Tags.Any(t => string.Equals(t, tag, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
    }

    public void ToggleTag(string tag)
    {
        if (HasTag(tag))
        {
            Tags.RemoveAll(t => string.Equals(t, tag, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
        }
        else
        {
            Tags.Add(tag);
        }
    }

    public void Save()
    {
        try
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(FolderPath) || !Directory.Exists(FolderPath)) return;
            string jsonPath = Path.Combine(FolderPath, "mod.json");
            File.WriteAllText(jsonPath, JsonConvert.SerializeObject(this, Formatting.Indented));
        }
        catch (Exception e)
        {
            LogService.LogWarning($"Failed to save mod.json: {e.Message}");
        }
    }
}

/// <summary>
/// Extensions for improving ease of use of ModDeclare.
/// </summary>
public static class ModDeclareExtensions
{
    public static Version ParseVersion(this ModDeclare pModDeclare)
    {
        try
        {
            var ver = Version.Parse(pModDeclare.Version);
            var major = Math.Max(0, ver.Major);
            var minor = Math.Max(0, ver.Minor);
            var build = Math.Max(0, ver.Build);
            var revision = Math.Max(0, ver.Revision);
            return new Version(major, minor, build, revision);
        }
        catch (Exception)
        {
            return new Version(0, 0, 0, 0);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Tries to get the declaration of a mod.
    /// Note that this method cannot reliably be used for precompiled NML mods, as there's no simple way to link their Assemblies to their ModDeclares.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="pModAssembly">The Assembly which a ModDeclare should be found for.</param>
    /// <param name="pModDeclare">The ModDeclare of the Assembly, if one was found.</param>
    /// <returns>Whether a ModDeclare could be matched to the provided Assembly.</returns>
    public static bool TryGetDeclaration(this Assembly pModAssembly, out ModDeclare pModDeclare)
    {
        foreach (var mod in WorldBoxMod.AllRecognizedMods.Keys)
        {
            switch (mod.ModType)
            {
                case ModTypeEnum.NEOMOD:
                    if (mod.UID == pModAssembly.GetName().Name)
                    {
                        pModDeclare = mod;
                        return true;
                    }

                    break;
                case ModTypeEnum.COMPILED_NEOMOD:
                    WorldBoxMod.TryGetLoadedMod(mod, out IMod modObj);
                    if (modObj != null)
                    {
                        if (pModAssembly == modObj.GetType().Assembly)
                        {
                            pModDeclare = mod;
                            return true;
                        }

                        if (pModAssembly.Modules.SelectMany(m => m.GetTypes())
                            .Where(t => t.GetInterfaces().Contains(typeof(IMod)))
                            .Any(modClass => modClass.IsInstanceOfType(modObj)))
                        {
                            pModDeclare = mod;
                            return true;
                        }
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        if (Directory.GetFiles(mod.FolderPath).Any(possible_file =>
                                Path.GetFullPath(possible_file) ==
                                Path.GetFullPath(pModAssembly.Location)))
                            /* It might be organized as following:
                             * -ModFolder
                             * |-mod.json
                             * |-main_assembly.dll
                             * |-submodule1.dll
                             * |-submodule2.dll
                             * ...
                             * Hope submodule can get mod's declaration successfully
                             */
                        {
                            pModDeclare = mod;
                            return true;
                        }

                        if (string.Concat(mod.Name.Where(c => new Regex(@"\S").IsMatch(c.ToString()))) ==
                            pModAssembly.GetName().Name)
                        {
                            pModDeclare = mod;
                            return true;
                        }
                    }

                    break;
                case ModTypeEnum.BEPINEX:
                    if (mod.Name == pModAssembly.GetCustomAttribute<AssemblyTitleAttribute>()?.Title)
                    {
                        pModDeclare = mod;
                        return true;
                    }

                    break;
                case ModTypeEnum.RESOURCE_PACK:
                    break;
                default:
                    throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();
            }
        }

        pModDeclare = null;
        return false;
    }
}

--- api/BasicMod.cs  (the authoritative base class + lifecycle doc) ---
using NeoModLoader.constants;
using NeoModLoader.services;
using UnityEngine;

namespace NeoModLoader.api;

/// <summary>
/// If you want to create a simple mod, you can inherit this class.
/// <para>Then NML will find this class in your compiled mod, then load it into ModLoader provided by WorldBox</para>
/// <para>You can get your mod's information by <see cref="GetDeclaration"/></para>
/// <para>You can get your mod's gameObject loaded to ModLoader by <see cref="GetGameObject"/></para>
/// <para>You must override <see cref="OnModLoad"/> to load your mod or add loading code to Awake and others will called automatically</para>
/// <remarks>
/// The common order of calling is:
/// OnModLoad -> Awake -> OnEnable -> Start -> Update
/// </remarks>
/// </summary>
public abstract class BasicMod<T> : MonoBehaviour, IMod, ILocalizable, IConfigurable, IFeatureLoadManaged, IStagedLoad
    where T : BasicMod<T>
{
    private ModConfig  _config  = null!;
    private ModDeclare _declare = null!;
    private bool       _isLoaded;
    private Transform  _prefab_library;

    /// <summary>
    /// Instance of your mod.
    /// </summary>
    public static T Instance { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>
    ///     Shortcut of <see cref="Instance" />
    /// </summary>
    public static T I => Instance;

    /// <summary>
    ///     A transform contains all prefabs of mods created through <see cref="NewPrefab(string)" />.
    /// </summary>
    public Transform PrefabLibrary
    {
        get
        {
            if (_prefab_library == null)
            {
                _prefab_library = transform.Find("PrefabLibrary");
                if (_prefab_library == null)
                {
                    _prefab_library = new GameObject("PrefabLibrary").transform;
                    _prefab_library.SetParent(transform);
                }
            }

            return _prefab_library;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    ///     Get the config of your mod.
    /// </summary>
    /// <returns>Config instance reference</returns>
    public ModConfig GetConfig()
    {
        return _config;
    }

    /// <summary>
    ///     An instance of the <see cref="IModFeatureManager" /> that is able to dynamically manage feature for this mod if
    ///     wanted.
    /// </summary>
    public IModFeatureManager ModFeatureManager { get; private set; }

    /// <summary>
    ///     If you need to add locale files for your mod, create locale files written by JSON under `Locales` directory in your
    ///     mod
    /// </summary>
    /// <returns>The path to the directory of your locale files</returns>
    public string GetLocaleFilesDirectory(ModDeclare pModDeclare)
    {
        return Path.Combine(pModDeclare.FolderPath, "Locales");
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Get the gameObject the mod attached to.
    /// </summary>
    public GameObject GetGameObject()
    {
        return gameObject;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Get the url set in mod.json or url of WorldBoxOpenMods' organization.
    /// </summary>
    public virtual string GetUrl()
    {
        return string.IsNullOrEmpty(_declare.RepoUrl) ? CoreConstants.OrgURL : _declare.RepoUrl;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Do not call this method manually, it is useless.
    /// <remarks>Unless you know what to do, like try loading again after an exception on <see cref="OnModLoad"/></remarks>
    /// </summary>
    public void OnLoad(ModDeclare pModDecl, GameObject pGameObject)
    {
        if (_isLoaded) return;
        _declare = pModDecl;
        Instance = (T)this;
        ModFeatureManager = new ModFeatureManager<T>(this);
        _config ??= LoadConfig();
        LogInfo("OnLoad");
        OnModLoad();
        ModFeatureManager.InstantiateFeatures();
        LogInfo("Loaded");
        _isLoaded = true;
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    /// <note>
    /// Calls Init() on the ModFeatureManager by default.
    /// </note>
    public virtual void Init()
    {
        ModFeatureManager.Init();
    }
    
    /// <inheritdoc />
    /// <note>
    /// Calls PostInit() on the ModFeatureManager by default.
    /// </note>
    public virtual void PostInit()
    {
        ModFeatureManager.PostInit();
    }

    /// <summary>
    ///     Get the gameObject the mod attached to.
    /// </summary>
    public ModDeclare GetDeclaration()
    {
        return _declare;
    }

    /// <summary>
    ///     Create a new raw prefab under the mod's prefab library.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="name"></param>
    /// <returns></returns>
    public static GameObject NewPrefab(string name)
    {
        var obj = new GameObject(name);
        obj.transform.SetParent(Instance.PrefabLibrary);
        return obj;
    }

    private ModConfig LoadConfig()
    {
        ModConfig persistent_config =
            new ModConfig(Path.Combine(Paths.ModsConfigPath, $"{_declare.UID}.config"), true);

        string default_config_path = Path.Combine(_declare.FolderPath, Paths.ModDefaultConfigFileName);
        if (!File.Exists(default_config_path))
        {
            return persistent_config;
        }

        var default_config =
            new ModConfig(Path.Combine(_declare.FolderPath, Paths.ModDefaultConfigFileName));
        persistent_config.MergeWith(default_config);
        persistent_config.ApplyCallbacks();

        return persistent_config;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// You should override this method to load your mod.
    /// </summary>
    protected abstract void OnModLoad();

    /// <summary>
    /// Log a message with mod name.
    /// </summary>
    public static void LogInfo(string message)
    {
        LogService.LogInfo($"[{Instance._declare.Name}]: {message}");
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Log a warning message with mod name.
    /// </summary>
    public static void LogWarning(string message)
    {
        LogService.LogWarning($"[{Instance._declare.Name}]: {message}");
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Log an error message with mod name.
    /// </summary>
    public static void LogError(string message)
    {
        LogService.LogError($"[{Instance._declare.Name}]: {message}");
    }
}

--- general/LM.cs  (the authoritative localization API) ---
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using HarmonyLib;
using NeoModLoader.api.exceptions;
using NeoModLoader.constants;
using NeoModLoader.services;
using Newtonsoft.Json;

namespace NeoModLoader.General;

/// <summary>
/// LM is short for Localization Manager
/// </summary>
public static class LM
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Store all locales loaded by NML.
    /// </summary>
    private static Dictionary<string, Dictionary<string, string>> locales = new();

    private static readonly Dictionary<string, string> str2esc = new()
    {
        {
            "\\n", "\n"
        },
        {
            "\\r", "\r"
        },
        {
            "\\t", "\t"
        },
        {
            "\\b", "\b"
        },
        {
            "\\f", "\f"
        },
        {
            "\\\"", "\""
        },
        {
            "\\\'", "\'"
        },
        {
            "\\\\", "\\"
        },
        {
            "\\0", "\0"
        }
    };

    /// <summary>
    /// Get localized text from key for current language
    /// </summary>
    /// <returns>Same with <see cref="LocalizedTextManager.getText"/></returns>
    [MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.AggressiveInlining | MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
    public static string Get(string key)
    {
        return LocalizedTextManager.getText(key);
    }

    /// <summary>
    ///     Check whether <paramref name="key" /> exists in <paramref name="lang" />
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="key"></param>
    /// <param name="lang">default: current language</param>
    /// <returns></returns>
    public static bool Has(string key, string lang = "")
    {
        return string.IsNullOrEmpty(lang)
            ? LocalizedTextManager.instance._localized_text.ContainsKey(key)
            : locales.TryGetValue(lang, out var dict) && dict.ContainsKey(key);
    }

    /// <summary>
    ///     Load locales from a file(Only support csv file)
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="pFilePath">Path to csv file</param>
    /// <param name="pSep">CSV seperation code</param>
    /// <exception cref="FormatException">Invalid csv file</exception>
    public static void LoadLocales(string pFilePath, char pSep = ',')
    {
        if (pFilePath.ToLower().EndsWith(".csv"))
        {
            Dictionary<string, Dictionary<string, string>> locale = null;
            try
            {
                locale = ParseCSV(File.ReadAllText(pFilePath), pSep);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                LogService.LogWarning($"Failed to load locale file at {pFilePath} as csv: {e.Message}");
                return;
            }

            if (locale == null)
            {
                LogService.LogWarning($"Failed to load locale file at {pFilePath} as csv");
                return;
            }

            foreach (var language in locale.Keys)
            {
                var dict = locale[language];
                foreach (var k in dict.Keys)
                {
                    Add(language, k, dict[k]);
                }
            }
        }
        else
        {
            LogService.LogWarning($"Unsupported locale file type of path: {pFilePath}");
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    ///     Load locales from a text stream(Only support csv text)
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="pStream">Stream of a csv text</param>
    /// <param name="pSep">CSV seperation code</param>
    /// <exception cref="FormatException">Invalid csv text</exception>
    public static void LoadLocales(Stream pStream, char pSep = ',')
    {
        string text = new StreamReader(pStream).ReadToEnd();
        Dictionary<string, Dictionary<string, string>> locale = null;
        try
        {
            locale = ParseCSV(text, pSep);
        }
        catch (Exception e)
        {
            LogService.LogWarning($"Failed to load locale text \"{text}\" as csv: {e.Message}");
            return;
        }

        if (locale == null)
        {
            LogService.LogWarning($"Failed to load locale text \"{text}\" as csv");
            return;
        }

        foreach (var language in locale.Keys)
        {
            var dict = locale[language];
            foreach (var k in dict.Keys)
            {
                Add(language, k, dict[k]);
            }
        }
    }

    private static Dictionary<string, Dictionary<string, string>> ParseCSV(string pText, char sep)
    {
        pText = pText.Replace("\r\n", "\n");
        var lines = pText.Split('\n');

        if (lines.Length < 2) return null;
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(lines[0].Trim())) return null;
        if (!lines[0].Contains(sep)) return null;

        var languages = lines[0].Split(sep);
        var locale = new Dictionary<string, Dictionary<string, string>>();
        for (int i = 1; i < languages.Length; i++)
        {
            locale[languages[i]] = new Dictionary<string, string>();
        }

        for (int i = 1; i < lines.Length; i++)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(lines[i].Trim())) continue;
            if (!lines[i].Contains(sep)) continue;
            var line = str2esc.Keys.Aggregate(lines[i], (current, key) => current.Replace(key, str2esc[key]))
                .Split(sep);
            var key = line[0];

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(key)) continue;
            if (line.Length > languages.Length) throw new Exception($"Line {i} has more '{sep}' than its head.");

            for (int j = 1; j < line.Length; j++)
            {
                locale[languages[j]][key] = line[j];
            }
        }

        return locale;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Load locale from a stream (It must be a json file)
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="pLanguage">Target save language</param>
    /// <param name="pStream">Stream of locale file</param>
    /// <exception cref="FormatException">Text in <see name="pStream"/> is not in correct format to its file name extension</exception>
    public static void LoadLocale(string pLanguage, Stream pStream)
    {
        string text = new StreamReader(pStream).ReadToEnd();
        Dictionary<string, string> locale =
            JsonConvert.DeserializeObject<Dictionary<string, string>>(text);

        if (locale == null)
        {
            throw new FormatException($"Failed to load locale file for stream as json");
        }

        foreach (var (key, value) in locale.Select<KeyValuePair<string, string>, (string key, string value)>(pair =>
                     (pair.Key, pair.Value)))
        {
            Add(pLanguage, key, value);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Load locale from a file
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="pLanguage">Target save language</param>
    /// <param name="pFilePath">Path to locale file</param>
    /// <exception cref="UnsupportedFileTypeException">Only support <see name="pFilePath"/> ends with ".json"</exception>
    /// <exception cref="FormatException">File at <see name="pFilePath"/> is not in correct format to its file name extension</exception>
    public static void LoadLocale(string pLanguage, string pFilePath)
    {
        if (pFilePath.ToLower().EndsWith(".json"))
        {
            Dictionary<string, string> locale =
                JsonConvert.DeserializeObject<Dictionary<string, string>>(File.ReadAllText(pFilePath));

            if (locale == null)
            {
                throw new FormatException($"Failed to load locale file at {pFilePath} as json");
            }

            foreach (var (key, value) in locale.Select<KeyValuePair<string, string>, (string key, string value)>(pair =>
                         (pair.Key, pair.Value)))
            {
                Add(pLanguage, key, value);
            }
        }
        /*
        else if (pFilePath.ToLower().EndsWith(".yml"))
        {

        }
        */
        else
        {
            LogService.LogWarning($"Unsupported locale file type of path: {pFilePath}");
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Add a key-value pair to current locale
    /// <remarks>Overwrite if key exists</remarks>
    /// </summary>
    [MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.AggressiveInlining | MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
    public static void AddToCurrentLocale(string key, string value)
    {
        LocalizedTextManager.instance._localized_text[key] = value;
        Add(LocalizedTextManager.instance.language, key, value);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Add a key-value pair to language locale
    /// <param name="language">Target language</param>
    /// <param name="key"></param>
    /// <param name="value"></param>
    /// <remarks>Overwrite if key exists</remarks>
    /// </summary>
    [MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.AggressiveInlining | MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
    public static void Add(string language, string key, string value)
    {
        if (!locales.ContainsKey(language))
        {
            locales[language] = new Dictionary<string, string>();
        }

        locales[language][key] = value;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Apply all locales loaded by this mod to target locale.
    /// <remarks>It will be called automatically by NML when language is changed.</remarks>
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="language">Language to apply</param>
    /// <param name="pUpdateTexts"></param>
    [MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
    public static void ApplyLocale(string language, bool pUpdateTexts = true)
    {
        if (!locales.ContainsKey(language))
        {
            locales[language] = new Dictionary<string, string>();
        }

        foreach (var (key, value) in locales[language]
                     .Select<KeyValuePair<string, string>, (string key, string value)>(pair => (pair.Key, pair.Value)))
        {
            LocalizedTextManager.instance._localized_text[key] = value;
        }

        foreach (var key in locales[CoreConstants.DefaultLocaleID].Keys
                     .Where(key =>
                         !LocalizedTextManager.instance._localized_text
                             .ContainsKey(key)))
            LocalizedTextManager.instance._localized_text[key] = locales[CoreConstants.DefaultLocaleID][key];

        if (pUpdateTexts) LocalizedTextManager.updateTexts();
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Apply all locales loaded by this mod to current locale.
    /// <remarks>It will be called automatically by NML when language is changed.</remarks>
    /// </summary>
    [MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.Synchronized)]
    public static void ApplyLocale(bool pUpdateTexts = true)
    {
        if (!locales.ContainsKey(LocalizedTextManager.instance.language))
        {
            locales[LocalizedTextManager.instance.language] = new Dictionary<string, string>();
        }

        foreach (var (key, value) in locales[LocalizedTextManager.instance.language]
                     .Select<KeyValuePair<string, string>, (string key, string value)>(pair => (pair.Key, pair.Value)))
        {
            LocalizedTextManager.instance._localized_text[key] = value;
        }

        foreach (var key in locales[CoreConstants.DefaultLocaleID].Keys
                     .Where(key =>
                         !LocalizedTextManager.instance._localized_text
                             .ContainsKey(key)))
            LocalizedTextManager.instance._localized_text[key] = locales[CoreConstants.DefaultLocaleID][key];

        if (pUpdateTexts) LocalizedTextManager.updateTexts();
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Restore mod translations before the game's language switch refreshes registered text fields.
    /// </summary>
    [HarmonyPostfix]
    [HarmonyPatch(typeof(LocalizedTextManager), nameof(LocalizedTextManager.loadLocalizedText))]
    internal static void loadLocalizedTextPostfix(string pLocaleID)
    {
        ApplyLocale(pLocaleID, false);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Patch to <see cref="LocalizedTextManager.setLanguage"/>
    /// <remarks>Listen language change event</remarks>
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="pLanguage">Parameter of <see cref="LocalizedTextManager.setLanguage"/></param>
    [HarmonyPostfix]
    [HarmonyPatch(typeof(LocalizedTextManager), nameof(LocalizedTextManager.setLanguage))]
    internal static void setLanguagePostfix(string pLanguage)
    {
        ApplyLocale(pLanguage);
    }
}

        }

            MetadataReference.CreateFromFile(Paths.PublicizedAssemblyPath));

    internal static HashSet<string> CompileModNodes(IEnumerable<ModDependencyNode> pModNodes, bool pForce = false)
    {
        List<ModDependencyNode> nodes = pModNodes.Distinct().ToList();
        HashSet<string> successful_nodes = new();
        if (nodes.Count == 0)
        {
            return successful_nodes;
        }

        CompileSessionContext context = CreateCompileSessionContext(nodes);
        List<List<ModDependencyNode>> stages =
            ModDependencyUtils.PartitionModsCompileStagesFromDependencyTopology(nodes);
        Dictionary<string, CompileNodeResult> completed_results = new();
        bool has_compile_failures = false;

        foreach (List<ModDependencyNode> stage in stages)
        {
            Dictionary<string, MetadataReference> available_mod_references =
                context.ModReferences.ToDictionary(entry => entry.Key, entry => entry.Value);
            ConcurrentDictionary<string, CompileNodeResult> stage_results = new();
            int max_degree_of_parallelism = Math.Min(stage.Count, Math.Max(1, Environment.ProcessorCount - 1));

            Parallel.ForEach(stage, new ParallelOptions { MaxDegreeOfParallelism = max_degree_of_parallelism }, node =>
            {
                stage_results[node.mod_decl.UID] =
                    CompileNodeInSession(node, context, available_mod_references, completed_results, pForce);
            });

            foreach (ModDependencyNode node in stage)
            {
                CompileNodeResult result = stage_results[node.mod_decl.UID];
                completed_results[node.mod_decl.UID] = result;

                if (result.Success)
                {
                    successful_nodes.Add(node.mod_decl.UID);
                    node.mod_decl.FailReason.Clear();

using System.Reflection;
using UnityEngine;

namespace NeoModLoader.constants;

/// <summary>
/// Common used paths
/// </summary>
public static class Paths
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Path to the mod loader file
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string NMLModPath;

    /// <summary>
    /// Path to persistent data
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string PersistentDataPath = Combine(Application.persistentDataPath);

    /// <summary>
    /// Path to folder StreamingAssets
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string StreamingAssetsPath = Combine(Application.streamingAssetsPath);

    /// <summary>
    /// Path to game native Mods folder
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string NativeModsPath = Others.IsMobile ? Combine(PersistentDataPath, "mods") : Combine(StreamingAssetsPath, "mods");

    /// <summary>
    /// Path to game native Managed folder
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string ManagedPath = Others.is_editor
        ? Combine(StreamingAssetsPath, "..", ".Managed")
        : Others.IsMobile ? Combine(PersistentDataPath, "mono") :Combine(StreamingAssetsPath, "..", "Managed");

    /// <summary>
    /// Path to folder contains NML's cache
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string NMLPath = Combine(NativeModsPath, "NML");

    /// <summary>
    ///     Path to file contains NML's commit hash
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string NMLCommitPath = Combine(NMLPath, "commit");

    /// <summary>
    ///     Path to the legacy auto update module. The updater has been removed;
    ///     NML only deletes this file on startup if an older install left it behind.
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string NMLAutoUpdateModulePath =
        Combine(NativeModsPath, "NeoModLoader.AutoUpdate_memload.dll");

    /// <summary>
    /// Path to the publicized Assembly-CSharp.dll file
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string PublicizedAssemblyPath = Combine(NMLPath, "Assembly-CSharp-Publicized.dll");

    /// <summary>
    /// Path to folder mods config under persistent data folder
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string ModsConfigPath = Combine(PersistentDataPath, "mods_config");
    
    public static readonly string PCInputConfigPath = Combine(PersistentDataPath, "PCInputConfig.json");
    /// <summary>
    /// Path to BepInEx plugins folder
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string BepInExPluginsPath = Combine(GamePath, "BepInEx", "plugins");

    /// <summary>
    /// Path to Mods folder provided by NML
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string ModsPath =
        Others.is_editor ? Combine(GamePath, "Assets", "Mods") : Combine(GamePath, "Mods");

    /// <summary>
    /// Path to extracted Assemblies cache
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string NMLAssembliesPath = Combine(NMLPath, "Assemblies");

    /// <summary>
    /// Path to compiled mods cache
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string CompiledModsPath = Combine(NMLPath, "CompiledMods");

    /// <summary>
    /// Path to tab order record file
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string TabOrderRecordPath = Combine(NMLPath, "tab_order_records.json");

    /// <summary>
    /// Path to mod compilation timestamps record file
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string ModCompileRecordPath = Combine(NMLPath, "mod_compile_records.json");

    /// <summary>
    /// Path to disabled mods record file
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string ModsDisabledRecordPath = Combine(NMLPath, "disabled_mods.txt");

    /// <summary>
    /// File name of a mod declaration
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string ModDeclarationFileName = "mod.json";

    /// <summary>
    /// File name of a default mod config file for BasicMod
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string ModDefaultConfigFileName = "default_config.json";

    /// <summary>
    /// Folder name of a mod's resource folder which will be patched to <see cref="Resources"/>
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string ModResourceFolderName = Others.is_editor ? "Resources" : "GameResources";

    /// <summary>
    /// Folder name of a NCMS mod's additional resource folder which will also be patched to <see cref="Resources"/>
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string NCMSAdditionModResourceFolderName = "GameResourcesReplace";

    /// <summary>
    /// Folder name of a mod's assetbundles.
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string ModAssetBundleFolderName = "AssetBundles";

    /// <summary>
    /// Path to the folder of game's workshop content
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string CommonModsWorkshopPath =
        Combine(GamePath, "..", "..", "workshop", "content", CoreConstants.GameId.ToString());

    /// <summary>
    /// Folder name of a NCMS mod's EmbeddedResource folder
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly string
        NCMSModEmbededResourceFolderName =
            "EmbededResources"; // note that this typo in "Embedded" has to stay, as NCMS also has it

    /// <summary>

    /// </summary>
    public static readonly HashSet<string> IgnoreSearchDirectories = new HashSet<string>()
    {
        "bin", "obj", "Properties", "packages", "packages.config", "packages-lock.json", "packages-lock.xml",
    };

    /// </summary>
    public static readonly HashSet<string> CompileIgnoreSearchDirectories = new HashSet<string>()
    {
        "bin", "obj", "Properties", "packages", "packages.config", "packages-lock.json", "packages-lock.xml",
        "GameResources", "GameResourcesReplace", "AssetBundles"
    };

    internal static readonly string LinuxSteamLocalConfigPath =
        "~/.local/share/Steam/userdata/{0}/config/localconfig.vdf";

    static Paths()
    {
        var nml_mod_path = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().Location;
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(nml_mod_path))
        {
            nml_mod_path = Combine(NativeModsPath, "NeoModLoader.dll");
            if (!File.Exists(nml_mod_path)) nml_mod_path = Combine(NativeModsPath, "NeoModLoader_memload.dll");
        }

        NMLModPath = nml_mod_path;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Path to game root folder
    /// </summary>
    public static string GamePath => Application.platform switch
    {
        RuntimePlatform.WindowsPlayer => Combine(StreamingAssetsPath, "..", ".."),
        RuntimePlatform.LinuxPlayer   => Combine(StreamingAssetsPath, "..", ".."),
        RuntimePlatform.OSXPlayer     => Combine(StreamingAssetsPath, "..", "..", "..", "..", ".."),
        RuntimePlatform.Android       => Combine(PersistentDataPath, ".."),
        RuntimePlatform.IPhonePlayer =>  Combine(PersistentDataPath, ".."),
        _                             => Combine(StreamingAssetsPath, "..", "..")
    };

    private static string Combine(params string[] paths) => new FileInfo(paths.Aggregate("", Path.Combine)).FullName;
}

--- Home(English).md ---
# Home

## Introduction

## How to install

## How to make mods

NeoModLoader supports loading/recognizing multiple mod formats, you can use any of them to make mods.

### Supported mod formats
1. Native mods that implement IMod
2. BepInEx plugins
3. NCMS mods

Here we mainly introduce the method of making native mods that implement IMod. Jump to [tutorial page](ModTutorialHome)

BepInEx tutorial see [Google Search](https://www.google.com/search?q=BepInEx+plugin+tutorial)

NCMS tutorial see [NCMS documentation](https://denq04.github.io/ncms/getting-started/)

NML\commit file content: 02266fe8185d940c4065185de0a117c18975cf09
Upstream commit 02266fe... date: 2024-10-02T08:53:26Z  msg: "Bugfix: Remove early usage of VirtualMod object"

Upstream branches: 0.22.9 (56c5cdd9876f572adeb14b88f250c10afe4ce78d), master

FINDING: the installed NeoModLoader.dll contains public types that exist in NEITHER upstream master
NOR the pinned commit 02266fe:
  NeoModLoader.utils.Builders.*          (MasterBuilder, ActorTraitBuilder, AssetBuilder`2, ...)
  NeoModLoader.utils.SerializedAssets.*  (SerializableAsset`1, SerializedActorTrait, SerializedItemAsset)

=> The installed build is NEWER than any public upstream commit found. INSTALLED DLL = GROUND TRUTH.

DLL exposes Paths.IgnoreSearchDirectories (renamed). The installed DLL was used throughout.

================================================================================
SECTION 17 - UNVERIFIED ITEMS (explicitly flagged, not asserted as fact)
================================================================================
1. The installed NeoModLoader.dll does not correspond to any public upstream commit (see SECTION 16).
   Its exact upstream origin is UNVERIFIED. Only the observable API was documented.
2. The exact C# PREPROCESSOR SYMBOL naming produced from Dependencies/OptionalDependencies is UNVERIFIED.
   (The symbol list is produced in the dependency-solver path, which was traced to
    ModDependencyUtils.ParseDepenNameToPreprocessSymbol but not fully read.)
3. Whether the installed build honours .gitignore when collecting .cs files is UNVERIFIED.
   Upstream master does (GitIgnoreUtils.GetRuleSet); presence in the installed DLL not confirmed.
4. The runtime CONSUMPTION path for GameResources/ , GameResourcesReplace/ and AssetBundles/ is UNVERIFIED.
   The folder-name constants and their exact casing are verified (Paths.*), but the code that
   copies them into the game was not traced.
5. ModConfig native support for the flat "id -> value" JSON style (e.g. SCPBOX_MOD\mod_settings.json) is
   UNVERIFIED. The group->items[] style in default_config.json IS verified from CoreBox.
6. SpriteLoadUtils / AssetBundleUtils / HarmonyUtils overload semantics are UNVERIFIED:
   signatures were extracted from metadata but nothing was executed.
7. The trait localization key shape "trait_<snake_id>" with suffix "_info" / "_info_2" is a
   HIGH-CONFIDENCE INFERENCE from Assembly-CSharp IL (BaseTrait.getLocaleID = typed_id + "_" + id,
   ActorTrait.get_typed_id() == "trait"; getDescriptionID appends "_info"). No literal in-game example was found.
8. The CSV locale header column semantics are UNVERIFIED (LM.ParseCSV inspected, but no .csv locale
   file exists in any installed mod).
9. Whether "name" is strictly mandatory in mod.json is UNVERIFIED in code: the deserialiser tolerates a
   missing name, but the UID fallback (Author + "." + Name) and the log prefix both depend on it.
10. The "main" key seen in StreamingAssets\mods\ElementalPowersMod\mod.json is not a ModDeclare property;
    whether some other NML path consumes it is UNVERIFIED.
11. The exact Harmony patch surface exposed by Assembly-CSharp-Publicized.dll was not enumerated
    (it is a re-publicised copy of Assembly-CSharp.dll, 22 MB).
